############################################################
##### NEW ANGMARRIM LINES - 5530 TO 7100 ###################
############################################################
# Three interwoven Angmarrim dynasties.
# Culture: angmarrim
# Faith: faith_angmar_evil
# Base-stat totals: 25-40 across diplomacy, martial, stewardship,
#                  intrigue, learning, and prowess.
# Main-line couples have 2-5 children.
# Spouses mix lowborn Angmarrim with periodic marriages among
# Houses Veikko, Kaarlo, and Erkki.
# Character names are drawn from names already used by Angmarrim
# characters in the supplied character-history file.
############################################################


############################################
##### HOUSE OF VEIKKO #####
############################################

tfw_lineofveikko_001 = { # Founder
	name = tfw_lineofveikko_001_name

	dynasty = dynasty_veikko
	religion = faith_angmar_evil
	culture = angmarrim

	trait = wrathful
	trait = paranoid
	trait = arbitrary

	trait = education_intrigue_2

	diplomacy = 4
	martial = 4
	stewardship = 4
	intrigue = 6
	learning = 5
	prowess = 8

		birth = yes
	}

	5555.11.12 = {
		add_spouse = tfw_lineoflowborn_angmarrim_001

	5594.12.15 = {
